- **star-schema-classifier**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 1. Classifies every column of a flat source table into one of five Kimball roles (fact_measure, dimension_attribute, dimension_fk, degenerate_dimension, date_time) and proposes a grain. Dispatched by the model-star-schema skill; not for direct user use. ([DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier](https://github.com/DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier/blob/HEAD/agents/star-schema-classifier.md))
- **star-schema-designer**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 2. Designs a complete Kimball-compliant star schema (fact table(s), dimensions with SK/BK/SCD, relationships, design notes) from classified columns. Honours validator feedback on retry. Dispatched by the model-star-schema skill; not for direct user use. ([DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier](https://github.com/DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier/blob/HEAD/agents/star-schema-designer.md))
- **star-schema-validator**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 3. Reviews a star schema against the nine Kimball rules (K-01…K-09), scores it 0–100, sets approved, and returns specific fixes. Dispatched by the model-star-schema skill; not for direct user use. ([DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier](https://github.com/DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier/blob/HEAD/agents/star-schema-validator.md))

- **stenographer**: Use when the user asks about past conversations, previous decisions, what was discussed before, or needs context from earlier sessions. - **step-1-labor-estimator**: Step 1 of AI labor disruption analysis. Researches and estimates the human labor % of revenue for a healthcare subsegment from 3 distinct sources, then writes findings to the Step 1 tab and populates the segment name on all tabs. - **step-2-task-inventor**: Step 2 of AI labor disruption analysis. - **step-3-atom-mapper**: Step 3 of AI labor disruption analysis. For each of the 12 tasks from Step 2, allocates 100% of time across the 12 workflow atom columns using the Appendix A boundary rules. Each row must sum exactly to 1.0. Writes to the Step 3 tab. ([vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence](https://github.com/vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence/blob/HEAD/agents/step-3-atom-mapper.md))
- **step-4-weight-assigner**: Step 4 of AI labor disruption analysis. Assigns labor time weights to each of the 12 tasks using O*NET ratings, BLS data, and industry benchmarks. Weights must sum to 1.0. Writes to the Step 4 Weighted Calc tab. ([vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence](https://github.com/vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence/blob/HEAD/agents/step-4-weight-assigner.md))
- **step-5-output-synthesizer**: Step 5 of AI labor disruption analysis. Reads the auto-calculated Final Output, runs a logic check, and writes a synthesized rationale per task explaining the automation ceiling outcome. Writes to the Final Output tab. ([vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence](https://github.com/vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence/blob/HEAD/agents/step-5-output-synthesizer.md))
- **step-6-regulatory-haircut**: Step 6 of AI labor disruption analysis. Discovers concrete, currently-in-force regulations that constrain projected automation savings for each of the 12 tasks, and assigns a per-task regulatory haircut %. Strict evidentiary bar — only real legislation with citations and URLs. Writes to the Step 6 Regulatory tab. ([vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence](https://github.com/vikMahen33/Agentic_Diligence/blob/HEAD/agents/step-6-regulatory-haircut.md))
- **step-back**: Use when you suspect the current approach is over-engineered, over-abstracted, or solving an imagined problem rather than the real one. - **step-executor**: Executes ONE BPE step as the worker for /bpe:goal autonomous mode. Backwards-compatible default with no mode argument runs the original bundled flow (implement + finalize in one dispatch). NEVER commit to main/master. NEVER use without a goal-driven parent. For interactive single-step work, use /bpe:execute-plan directly instead of dispatching this agent. ([bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team](https://github.com/bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team/blob/HEAD/agents/step-verifier.md))
- **step0-module-agent**: Step 0 of the SDLC pipeline. Takes a large, unstructured problem statement from a human and decomposes it into independently-runnable Modules, each of which will separately go through the full 14-step chain. Runs once per project (or once per major new initiative). Invoke this agent first, before any other pipeline agent, whenever a new project or a large new body of work is starting. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step0-module-agent.md))
- **step0b-solution-architecture-agent**: Runs once per project, immediately after the Module Agent's modules.md is Approved, and before any module proceeds past Step 5 (Test Scenarios) in its own chain. Decides the system architecture that Module Agent's business decomposition did not: tech stack, deployment topology, how modules actually communicate, and where shared concerns (auth, logging) live. Uses the C4 model (Context + Container diagrams) for what the architecture looks like, and Architecture Decision Records — the same format already used throughout this pipeline — for why. Invoke once /modules/modules.md is Approved. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step0b-solution-architecture-agent.md))
- **step1-business-requirements-agent**: Step 1 of the SDLC pipeline. Runs once per module, after the Module Agent has scaffolded the module folder. Captures the business need as a set of Business Requirements (BRs) — big, complete units of business capability, not fragmented micro-requirements. Invoke this agent when a module's folder exists and is ready for its business requirements to be captured. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step1-business-requirements-agent.md))
- **step1-resume-draft**: Creates an initial tailored resume draft from master resume materials and job description. This is Step 1 of the three-step resume creation process. The agent analyzes job requirements, applies cultural profile preferences, and uses the HAM-Z methodology to create a targeted first draft. Always presents cultural profile selection menu before proceeding. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step1-resume-draft.md) · ★25)
- **step1-resume-revise**: Revises an existing base resume toward a new job description using a change manifest and surgical edits. This is the revise-mode Step 1 of the three-step resume process. The agent performs gap analysis between the base resume and the job description, writes an explicit change manifest in which every change is justified by a named JD requirement, copies the base resume, applies only the manifest changes as targeted edits, and verifies with a diff gate that all unchanged content remains byte-identical to the base. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step1-resume-revise.md) · ★25)
- **step10-test-automation-agent**: Step 10 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every test scenario, one at a time, and turns it into an actual automated test at the layer the scenario was tagged for in Step 5. Invoke once 09-implementation.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step10-test-automation-agent.md))
- **step11-test-execution-agent**: Step 11 of the SDLC pipeline. Runs the automated test suite from Step 10 against the implementation from Step 9, and records real results — pass, fail, and the actual evidence (output/logs), not a summary written from memory. Invoke once 10-test-automation.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step11-test-execution-agent.md))
- **step12-improvement-agent**: Step 12 of the SDLC pipeline. Resolves every Fail routed from Test Execution, fixing exactly the failure and nothing more. Invoke once 11-test-execution.md is Sealed (or has routed blockers) for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step12-improvement-agent.md))
- **step13-monitoring-agent**: Step 13 of the SDLC pipeline. Sets up production monitoring for this module against the performance thresholds defined in Step 8, using control bands (rolling baseline + tiered response) rather than a single static alert. This is also the agent that closes the loop — a breached band writes a new BR-style intent for Step 1, restarting the pipeline for the affected module. Invoke once 12-improvement.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step13-monitoring-agent.md))
- **step14-deploy-docs-agent**: Step 14 of the SDLC pipeline. Maintains this module's release record using the Keep a Changelog format — a running "Unreleased" section that accumulates entries continuously from Implementation and Improvement, only sealed into a dated version number at actual release time. Not a one-shot document written at the very end. Invoke continuously as Implementation/Improvement items complete, and finally at release. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step14-deploy-docs-agent.md))
- **step2-functional-requirements-agent**: Step 2 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every approved Business Requirement in a module, one at a time, and produces the full set of Functional Requirements (FRs) that fulfil it. This is where detailed splitting happens — BRs stay coarse, FRs get granular. Invoke this agent once 01-business-requirements.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step2-functional-requirements-agent.md))
- **step2-provenance-check**: Performs comprehensive provenance analysis on Step 1 resume drafts to identify credibility issues, evidence gaps, and risk factors. Uses the CV Provenance Hardening methodology to create detailed analysis reports with specific recommendations for strengthening resume credibility before final draft creation. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step2-provenance-check.md) · ★25)
- **step3-final-resume**: Creates the final hardened resume by incorporating all recommendations from Step 2 provenance analysis. This agent takes the Step 1 draft and Step 2 analysis to produce a credible, defensible final resume that maintains competitive positioning while addressing all credibility concerns and evidence gaps. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step3-final-resume.md) · ★25)
- **step3-ux-agent**: Step 3 of the SDLC pipeline. First builds the module's full screen inventory — every screen the FRs imply, PLUS the prerequisite/scaffolding screens (signup, login, onboarding, splash, forgot-password, etc.) that business-level requirements routinely never spell out — and prioritizes every screen by feature importance. Then loops over every screen, one at a time, producing flows, structure, states, low-fidelity wireframes (element/button-level), a motion/transition model, and accessibility notes — grounded in deep, per-item research (the full BR/FR corpus, every project source document, live… ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step3-ux-agent.md))
- **step4-cover-letter**: Creates a compelling cover letter with requirements-matching table based on the final Step 3 resume ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step4-cover-letter.md) · ★25)
- **step4-cover-letter-forward**: Creates a forward-facing cover letter proposing a dual-anchored first-90-days plan (evidenced problem + past proof) based on the final Step 3 resume and a skill-level intake interview ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step4-cover-letter-forward.md) · ★25)
- **step4-ui-agent**: Step 4 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every approved UX flow/screen (FR-derived and prerequisite alike), one at a time, and produces the visual design spec — components, layout, tokens, themes, cosmetics, brand compliance, and a concrete motion/animation implementation of the UX agent's transition model — grounded in deep, per-screen research (the full BR/FR/UX corpus, every project source document, live internet research, and named real mobile-app references) rather than invented from habit. Screen Priority (P0/P1/P2, set by Step 3) governs how much polish/motion investment a screen gets.… ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step4-ui-agent.md))
- **step5-test-scenarios-agent**: Step 5 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every FR (and its UX/UI), one at a time, and writes test scenarios covering functional and UI/interaction behavior, written before implementation exists. Tags each scenario by test-pyramid layer so the eventual automation isn't accidentally e2e-heavy. Invoke once 04-ui.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step5-test-scenarios-agent.md))
- **step6-impact-analysis-agent**: Step 6 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every FR, one at a time, and performs change impact analysis in two distinct parts: dependency identification, then risk assessment against those dependencies. This is the second (and last) point in the pipeline where a "worth check" — should this proceed at all, given what we now know — is asked. Invoke once 05-test-scenarios.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step6-impact-analysis-agent.md))
- **step7-tech-reqs-er-model-agent**: Step 7 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every impact-analyzed FR and produces technical requirements. Does NOT own the ER model — that is Step 7a's (step7a-er-model-agent) exclusive responsibility; Step 7 hands off to it once Sealed, and Step 7 itself does not seal until Step 8 begins, it seals on its own Tech-Reqs-only Definition of Done. Invoke once 06-impact-analysis.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step7-tech-reqs-er-model-agent.md))
- **step7a-er-model-agent**: Step 7a of the SDLC pipeline. Dedicated ER model design and database implementation agent. Produces a comprehensive, fully-traceable ER model through a mandatory three-pass internal process (draft, cross-validate, sign-off), then implements the actual database schema, migrations, and initialization scripts. This agent owns all database structural decisions and implementation. Invoke once 07-tech-reqs.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step7a-er-model-agent.md))
- **step8-security-performance-agent**: Step 8 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every technical requirement, one at a time, and applies STRIDE threat modeling plus performance threshold analysis before any implementation exists — this is the shift-left security gate, not a post-implementation audit. Invoke once 07-tech-reqs.md and 07a-er-model.md are both Sealed/Approved for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step8-security-performance-agent.md))
- **step9-implementation-agent**: Step 9 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every technical requirement, one at a time, and implements it — but only after writing a requirement-level comment block stating intent and reasoning for that requirement's code. No code is written until its comment is complete. Invoke once 08-security-performance.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step9-implementation-agent.md))

- **step_3_search_plan**: &gt; 基于大纲和关键词，生成结构化检索方案。L1→L2→L3 分层路由架构 + 概念块布尔模型 + 反模式检查。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_3_search_plan.md) · ★30)
- **step_4_search_score**: &gt; 按 Step 3 方案的 L1→L2→L3 分层路由逐子课题检索，对结果进行 5 维度评分和 Tier 分级筛选。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_4_search_score.md) · ★30)
- **step_5_download**: &gt; ⚠️ **前置提醒：** &gt; 1. 进入真实下载前，如命中机构登录场景，agent 必须先提示用户在**同一个 CDP 浏览器**里完成机构登录，再继续执行下载。 &gt; 2. 如果宿主无法读取 `input()`，不得把下载脚本丢到后台硬跑；必须保留 `paper-temp/login_checkpoint.json`，并把该状态视为待用户确认登录，不是用户主动 skip。 &gt; 3. 用户完成机构登录后，恢复路径固定为 `python3 scripts/unified_download_router.py --resume-login-checkpoint paper-temp/login_checkpoint.json --output paper-temp/ --confirmed`。 &gt; 4. **未先问用户登录就直接启动真实下载是错误执行方式，不是 skill 缺陷。** &gt; &gt; ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_5_download.md) · ★30)
- **step_6_entry**: 本文件是 Step 6 的新入口层，不替代 [step_6_zotero.md](./step_6_zotero.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_6_entry.md) · ★30)

- **step_8_polishing**: &gt; 对既有成稿进行成稿级风险收口：全文一致性、术语终验、表达清理，以及**受约束补写 + 直接修改 + 修订后验证**。 &gt; Step 8 不负责主体写作，不补外部证据，不替代完整引用审计或修稿路线图；但它可以在现有正文、Step 7 三工件和评审/审计输入支撑下，对正文做修订性补写与局部增强。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_8_polishing.md) · ★30)

- **stepwise**: Teach ONE dense document to a newcomer by running the stepwise skill faithfully.
